# Jewelcrafting internal recipe cards

**DRAFT — exact review pending.** Internal: all 43 formulas, including one restricted and sixteen Director-only records.

Every card uses the complete [shared guide](JEWELCRAFTING_INTERNAL_GUIDE.md). Full preserved source objects accompany the JSON.

## recipe-241 — Field Veiled Bracelet

[[PARAGRAPH P1]]

[[PARAGRAPH P2]]

**Operational effect:** While complete, at positive structural HP, unsuppressed, unmelded, fitted and actually worn in one designated legal wrist-adornment position, the actual wearer gains +1 once on each independently qualifying Stealth-related ability check or saving throw under the exact predicates below. This passive benefit is not spent and remains with zero charges. This physical item has maximum 1 charges. A conscious, non-Incapacitated wearer may voluntarily spend one before an eligible roll commits to gain advantage on that event, with no separate Action, Bonus Action, Reaction, RP, speech or hand use. Declare before any die or replacement result; no retrospective reroll. At most one Jewelcrafting charge is spent per event, and ordinary advantage/disadvantage cancellation applies. The spending item must independently qualify but need not supply the strongest flat modifier. Ability-check modifiers share equipment.leather-task-enhancement with independently qualified Leatherworking/Tailoring; save modifiers share equipment.jewel-save-enhancement with other jewels. Use the strongest within the applicable family once. Charges initialize once at lawful creation and refill only at legitimate Jewelcrafting dawn events under the shared guide. Transfer, equipping, repair, suppression ending, new scenes and copied records never refill or reinitialize them. This grants no AC candidate, even zero; no resistance, attack modifier, proficiency, extra hand, focus permission or carrying relief.

SOURCE-BOUND INTERFACE WALKTHROUGHS AND16-DECISION COVERAGE

# Jewelcrafting interface walkthroughs — exact review evidence

These are manuscript walkthroughs, not claims of an implemented companion runtime. Recipe and item statistics below come from the saved candidate or existing reviewed records. Physical fit, custody, actual surroundings and placement legality are stated scenario premises. The source conference is authoring authority only; all 43 cards still await exact review.

## A hidden jewel and a contributing garment

Recipe-241 is a +1 Stealth Bracelet, not an armor item. Recipe-584 is a +3 Tailoring Garment with radiant resistance. An actual wearer makes a legitimate DEX (Stealth) check. Their fitted Bracelet remains active beneath compatible clothing. If the Garment actually provides useful visual concealment under its own reviewed predicate, compare 1 and 3 and apply 3 once. If its outline/coverage does not contribute, apply the independently qualifying Bracelet's 1. The Garment does not borrow the Bracelet's eligibility. Its separately qualified radiant resistance is independent of that comparison. The executable fixture supplies each item's own evidence.

## Rock climbing with gloves and an amulet

Recipe-306 is +2 Leatherworking Gloves. Recipe-649 is a +3 Earth Amulet. With actual compatible wrist/neck clearance and a legitimate STR (Athletics) climb using real rock holds, both independently qualify and the shared check family supplies 3, not 5. If only the glove-grip task qualifies, supply 2. The Amulet supplies no bonus to a merely nearby ally. A concentration save is excluded from Jewelcrafting even when the triggering damage was magical. Neither older trade gains a save route.

## Custody, meld, external priority and return

Use an ordinary fitted recipe-244 Crown: body value5 gp, mass0.5 lb, structural HP5/AC19, +1 Poison, maximum1 charge. It has no printed Load, so the existing body rule assigns provisional Load0.05 from its documented body mass. The actual wearer legitimately borrows it and records its head position before a lawful meld. The lender remains owner; the wearer has actual custody. It stays one physical item with one charge ledger.

Meld preflight counts its full0.5 lb and0.05 Load against the already established chain ceilings. While melded it is inaccessible and provides no modifier or charge activation. At a legitimate dawn it refills internally only if complete, positive-HP and unsuppressed. Actual suppression scope governs whether a particular suppression reaches it. This does not create access through the meld.

Suppose an independently acquired, genuinely compatible external Tailoring Hat occupies the head position when root anatomy returns. That external Hat has priority. The Crown cannot replace it or automatically designate a different position. In this scenario the root body's actual carried capacity and ordinary storage admit the Crown, so it returns carried and inactive with the same ledger. A later change requires the lawful equipping/removal Actions and actual fit. If no legal carrying or same-square/adjacent placement is available, the governing return procedure retains the single unresolved object in pending placement. Its actual elevation is preserved; mandatory reversion is not prevented, and ordinary falling is resolved under its existing procedure. No object vanishes, doubles or silently teleports to ground level.

A destroyed, transferred or irrecoverably lost Crown does not return from ownership metadata. A returning intact borrowed Crown in continuing actual custody does. Actual custody and current physical state, not a historical inventory copy, decide.

For an explicitly synthetic carrying arithmetic fixture, assume the transformed body's documented mass is10 lb and its governing casualty Load is1.5. With only this melded Crown, a rescuer must handle10.5 lb and1.55 Load. These supplied body values are scenario premises, not a new universal form profile. The Crown itself has the actual source-bound mass/Load above. Its meld supplies no relief to the rescuer.

## Original Gem, host and spent charges

Recipe-268 is a +2 Fire mounted Gem, maximum3 charges, body0.05 lb with ordinary HP5/AC13. Its actual supporting garment remains a separate object. Merely suppressing that garment's magic does not detach the pin or destroy physical support. Removing or destroying the actual support can end the Gem's worn state; the Gem still exists at its actual location.

After two lawful committed expenditures its ledger shows1 remaining. It breaks to structural HP0. A dawn while Broken is processed without refill. Trained reconstruction requires the original crystal and original primary mounting, the actual kit/bench/files/lens and the recipe DC. Missing either primary constituent defeats this route. A subordinate ordinary pin may be replaced as a repair material. Successful repair restores recorded structural maximum and otherwise legal usability but leaves1 charge and the processed missed dawn unchanged. A later legitimate eligible dawn can set it to3. No fragment yields a second jewel, and no temporary new item ID resets the ledger.

## Creation at dawn and a damaged singleton

A source-valid recipe-242 Earring has one charge and is one piece. A mundane HP1/5 reserve can resolve singleton-Flawed at HP0/2 while its first lawful imprint still initializes the original maximum1 charge once. It cannot use that charge while Broken. Repair can make the same already-recorded charge usable; repair did not refill it.

If creation occurs at the same clock timestamp as dawn, the campaign's actual event order matters. Initialization records which dawn the authoritative campaign scheduler has already processed. A newly created item cannot spend and then replay an already processed dawn merely because both share a timestamp. The reference requires that checkpoint explicitly. Displayed time, transfer, rest and initiative never reset it.

 'Psychic':[('INT','Arcana','examine-actual-psychic-mental-magic'),('INT','Investigation','permitted-altered-perception-reality-test')],
 'Arcane':[('INT','Arcana','examine-actual-magic-inscription-mechanism'),('CASTING','*','required-counteract-dispel-check')],
 'Cold':[('INT','Nature','assess-cold-ice'),('WIS','Survival','manage-avoid-cold-exposure'),('DEX','Acrobatics','specifically-icy-footing'),('CON','*','governing-cold-exposure-endurance')],
 'Vitality':[('WIS','Medicine','permitted-diagnosis-first-aid-stabilization-treatment'),('CON','*','bodily-exertion-exposure-deprivation-endurance')],
 'Lightning':[('INT','Nature','assess-electricity'),('INT','Arcana','assess-magical-electricity'),('WIS','Survival','present-electrical-hazard'),('DEX','*','required-noncraft-energized-manipulation')],
 'Fire':[('INT','Nature','assess-flame-heat'),('INT','Arcana','assess-magical-flame-heat'),('WIS','Survival','present-fire-heat-hazard'),('STR','*','actual-fire-containment-extinguishing'),('DEX','*','actual-fire-containment-extinguishing'),('CON','*','heat-endurance')],
 'Water':[('INT','Nature','assess-water-currents-route'),('WIS','Survival','assess-water-currents-route'),('STR','Athletics','swim'),('DEX','Acrobatics','liquid-water-hazardous-footing')],
 'Acid':[('INT','Nature','assess-acid-corrosion'),('INT','Arcana','assess-magical-acid-corrosion'),('WIS','Survival','present-acid-exposure'),('DEX','*','difficult-noncraft-acid-vessel-handling')]}
SAVES={
 'Stealth':['explicit-detection-revelation-concealment-resistance'],
 'Flora':['direct-binding-obstructing-plants','actual-botanical-spore-toxin-exposure'],
 'Poison':['direct-poison-damage-condition-exposure-consequence'],
 'Unholy':['necrotic-damage','operative-curse','documented-undead-magical-charm-fear-command'],
 'Earth':['direct-earth-stone-collapse-movement-crushing','explicit-petrification'],
 'Strength':['physical-force-movement-prone-restraint'],
 'Psychic':['psychic-damage','magical-charm-fear','explicit-thought-memory-mental-intrusion-command'],
 'Arcane':['direct-resolved-spell','direct-expressly-magical-effect'],
 'Cold':['cold-damage','direct-freezing-chilling-exposure'],
 'Vitality':['disease-poison-wound-injury-bodily-exposure-suffocation-exertion'],
 'Lightning':['lightning-damage','explicit-electrical-shock'],
 'Fire':['fire-damage','direct-burning-heat'],
 'Water':['direct-water-pressure-current-engulfment-drowning'],
 'Acid':['acid-damage','actual-explicit-corrosive-effect']}

def validate_designations(items):
    occupied={};hands=set();seen=set();counts={}
    for r in items:
        if r['id'] in seen:raise ValueError('duplicate physical identity')
        seen.add(r['id'])
        if r.get('state')!='worn' or not r.get('slotValid'):continue
        slot=r.get('slot');wearer=r.get('wearer');key=(wearer,slot)
        if key in occupied:raise ValueError('competing designated benefits')
        occupied[key]=r['id']
        if r.get('trade')!='Jewelcrafting':continue
        if slot not in SLOTS[r['family']]:raise ValueError('wrong jewel position')
        if not r.get('physicalCompatibilityEstablished'):raise ValueError('physical fit/anatomy/clearance not established')
        counts[wearer]=counts.get(wearer,0)+1
        if counts[wearer]>7:raise ValueError('too many active jewels')
        if r['family']=='Ring':
            hand=r.get('actualCompatibleHand')
            if not hand or (wearer,hand) in hands:raise ValueError('rings require different compatible hands')
            hands.add((wearer,hand))
    return occupied

def predicate_eligible(item,event):
    if not event.get('actualRoll') or event.get('automaticResult'):return False
    if set(event.get('categories',())).intersection(EXCLUSIONS):return False
    own=event.get('itemPremises',{}).get(item['id'],{})
    if not own.get('actualCausalPredicateEstablished'):return False
    predicates=set(own.get('predicateIds',()))
    tag=item['tag'];kind=event.get('kind');ability=event.get('ability');skill=event.get('proficiency')
    if kind=='ability-check':
        for a,s,p in CHECKS[tag]:
            amatch=ability==a if a!='CASTING' else event.get('actualSpellcastingAbilityCheck') is True
            if amatch and (s=='*' or skill==s) and p in predicates:return True
        return False
    if kind=='saving-throw':
        if tag=='Strength' and ability!='STR':return False
        if tag=='Vitality' and ability!='CON':return False
        return bool(predicates.intersection(SAVES[tag]))
    return False

def qualifies(item,event):
    return (item.get('trade')=='Jewelcrafting' and item.get('state')=='worn'
      and item.get('complete') and item.get('hp',0)>0 and not item.get('suppressed')
      and item.get('fit') and item.get('slotValid') and item.get('wearer')==event.get('actor')
      and item.get('physicalCompatibilityEstablished')
      and (item['family'] not in ('Brooch','Gem') or item.get('actualHostSupport'))
      and predicate_eligible(item,event))

def jewel_flat_bonus(items,event):
    validate_designations(items)
    return max((r['bonus'] for r in items if qualifies(r,event)),default=0)

def combined_check_bonus(items,event,older_trade_task,leather_task_ids=()):
    validate_designations(items)
    if event.get('kind')!='ability-check':raise ValueError('older trades have no save route')
    if older_trade_task.get('actor')!=event.get('actor') or older_trade_task.get('ability')!=event.get('ability') or older_trade_task.get('proficiency')!=event.get('proficiency'):raise ValueError('not the same actual check')
    others=[r for r in items if r.get('trade')!='Jewelcrafting']
    return max(jewel_flat_bonus(items,event),tailor.cross_trade_task_bonus(others,older_trade_task,leather_task_ids))

def new_ledger(campaign,epoch):
    return {'campaignId':campaign,'epoch':epoch,'items':{},'rollSpenders':{},'rollEffects':{}}

def initialize(ledger,item,creation_id,created_at,*,processed_dawn_through):
    if item['id'] in ledger['items']:raise ValueError('physical imprint already initialized')
    maximum=item['maximumCharges']
    if not isinstance(maximum,int) or not 1<=maximum<=6:raise ValueError('invalid source charge maximum')
    # The authoritative campaign scheduler supplies its already-processed
    # checkpoint, especially when creation coincides with a dawn timestamp.
    n=(created_at-ledger['epoch'])//86400
    allowed={None} if n<0 else {n}
    if created_at==ledger['epoch']+n*86400 and n>=0:allowed.add(n-1 if n else None)
    if processed_dawn_through not in allowed:raise ValueError('creation lacks correct campaign dawn checkpoint')
    d=deepcopy(ledger);d['items'][item['id']]={'recipeId':item['recipeId'],'maximum':maximum,'current':maximum,'creationId':creation_id,'createdAt':created_at,'createdAfterProcessedDawn':processed_dawn_through,'lastDawn':processed_dawn_through,'dawnEvents':[],'spends':[]};return d

def commit_charge(ledger,items,item_id,event):
    validate_designations(items)
    chosen=next((r for r in items if r['id']==item_id),None)
    if not chosen or not qualifies(chosen,event):raise ValueError('ineligible item or event')
    if not event.get('conscious') or event.get('incapacitated') or not event.get('voluntaryActivationAllowed'):raise ValueError('voluntary activation unavailable')
    if event.get('phase')!='before-commit' or event.get('resultRevealed'):raise ValueError('too late')
    if not event.get('id'):raise ValueError('missing actual roll identity')
    if event['id'] in ledger['rollSpenders']:raise ValueError('event already has a Jewelcrafting expenditure')
    state=ledger['items'].get(item_id)
    if not state or state['recipeId']!=chosen['recipeId'] or state['maximum']!=chosen['maximumCharges'] or state['current']<1:raise ValueError('charge unavailable or imprint mismatch')
    if event.get('canceledBeforeCommit'):return deepcopy(ledger)
    d=deepcopy(ledger);d['items'][item_id]['current']-=1;d['items'][item_id]['spends'].append(event['id']);d['rollSpenders'][event['id']]=item_id
    d['rollEffects'][event['id']]={'advantageGranted':True,'sourcePhysicalItem':item_id,'sameEventSecondExpenditureAllowed':False,'scope':'ordinary cancellation and replacement procedure govern dice'}
    return d

def dawn(ledger,item_id,index,at_time,actual_state_at_event):
    if not isinstance(index,int) or index<0 or at_time!=ledger['epoch']+index*86400:raise ValueError('not an authoritative dawn event')
    s=ledger['items'][item_id]
    if at_time<s['createdAt']:raise ValueError('dawn predates imprint')
    if s['lastDawn'] is not None and index<=s['lastDawn']:raise ValueError('replayed or out-of-order dawn')
    if actual_state_at_event.get('id')!=item_id or actual_state_at_event.get('recordedAt')!=at_time:raise ValueError('missing actual event-state history')
    if any(k not in actual_state_at_event for k in ('complete','hp','suppressed','primaryDestroyed')):raise ValueError('incomplete historical state')
    if s['lastDawn'] is not None and index!=s['lastDawn']+1:raise ValueError('resolve intervening dawn states first')
    first=max(0,ceil(F(s['createdAt']-ledger['epoch'],86400)))
    if s['lastDawn'] is None and index!=first:raise ValueError('resolve first elapsed dawn before later events')
    state=actual_state_at_event
    eligible=(state.get('complete') and state.get('hp',0)>0 and not state.get('suppressed') and not state.get('primaryDestroyed'))
    d=deepcopy(ledger);z=d['items'][item_id];z['lastDawn']=index;z['dawnEvents'].append({'index':index,'time':at_time,'refilled':bool(eligible),'state':deepcopy(state)})
    if eligible:z['current']=z['maximum']
    return d

def complete_order(reserves,recipe,outcome,option,declared_ids):
    """Latest-state inputs must retain their predeclared physical ordering."""
    if [r['id'] for r in reserves]!=declared_ids:raise ValueError('changed reserve order or identity')
    expected='jewelcrafting.'+('mounted-gem' if recipe['recipeFamily']=='Gem' else recipe['recipeFamily'].lower())
    for r in reserves:
        if r.get('model')!=expected or r.get('grade')!='Standard' or r.get('size') not in ('Tiny','Small','Medium','Large'):raise ValueError('unadmitted body')
        if r.get('complete') is not True or r.get('available') is not True or r.get('otherMagicalAlteration'):raise ValueError('body premise missing')
        ordinary=10 if r['size']=='Large' else 5
        if r.get('ordinaryHP')!=ordinary or r.get('maximum')!=structural_maximum(ordinary,r['percent'],r['flaw']):raise ValueError('body structural record inconsistent')
    if option not in ('yield','durability'):raise ValueError('no charge improvement option')
    return leather.complete_order(reserves,recipe['quantity'],outcome,option)

def catalyst_return(recipe,actual_units,outcome,selected_ids):
    """Select the one actual participant returned; not a harvest/aging engine."""
    if len(selected_ids)>1 or len(set(u['id'] for u in actual_units))!=len(actual_units):raise ValueError('nonunique unit or too many returns')
    if not selected_ids:return []
    if outcome!='Masterwork':raise ValueError('only actual Masterwork returns catalyst')
    selected=[u for u in actual_units if u['id'] in selected_ids]
    if len(selected)!=1:raise ValueError('actual unit absent')
    u=selected[0];i=u['componentIndex']
    if u.get('recipeId')!=recipe['id'] or not isinstance(i,int) or not 0<=i<len(recipe['components']):raise ValueError('wrong recipe participation')
    component=recipe['components'][i]
    if component['role']!='catalyst' or not component['consumed'] or u.get('componentId')!=component['id']:raise ValueError('not an actual eligible catalyst occurrence')
    if not u.get('lawfullyPreservedParticipant') or u.get('alreadyDestroyed'):raise ValueError('cannot manufacture a replacement participant')
    return deepcopy(selected)

def structural_maximum(ordinary,percent=0,flawed=False):
    if ordinary not in (5,10) or percent not in (0,25,50):raise ValueError('unadmitted structural premise')
    improved=ordinary+(max(1,ordinary*percent//100) if percent else 0)
    return max(1,improved//2) if flawed else improved

def quality_structure(ordinary,old_percent,old_flawed,old_current,new_percent=0,add_flaw=False):
    oldmax=structural_maximum(ordinary,old_percent,old_flawed)
    if not 1<=old_current<=oldmax:raise ValueError('reserve must be functional at completion')
    percent=max(old_percent,new_percent);flaw=old_flawed or add_flaw;maximum=structural_maximum(ordinary,percent,flaw)
    return {'maximum':maximum,'current':max(0,min(maximum,maximum-(oldmax-old_current))),'durabilityPercent':percent,'flawed':flaw}

def repair_quote(item,base_value,finished_value,recipe_dc,premises):
    """Return a quotation only after supplied original-assembly/tool gates.
    No clock, material inventory, actual work or charge ledger is mutated.
    """
    required=['trained','actualToolkit','actualBench','actualFineFiles','actualLens','recoverableOriginalPrimary','requiredOriginalStonesSettingsPresent']
    if any(premises.get(k) is not True for k in required):raise ValueError('missing actual repair premise')
    if premises.get('ability') not in ('DEX','INT'):raise ValueError('choose DEX or INT')
    if item['family']=='Gem' and not (premises.get('originalCrystal') and premises.get('originalPrimaryMount')):raise ValueError('original Gem assembly missing')
    return leather.repair_terms(item['hp'],item['maximumHP'],base_value,finished_value,recipe_dc,recoverable=True)


UNCHANGED REVIEWED TAILORING DEPENDENCY

"""Bounded manuscript calculations, not companion-runtime implementation.

Actual task circumstances, physical fit, anatomy, tool equivalence and board
placement are explicit premises established by their governing procedures.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import ceil
import leatherworking_reference as lw
from build_tailoring_recipe_candidate import FAMILY_TASKS, SLOTS

def inventory(records,bearer):
    transformed=deepcopy(records)
    for r in transformed:
        if r.get('family')=='Bag':
            cap=r['bodyCapacityLb']
            if cap not in (10,30,40,50):raise ValueError('unadmitted Bag model')
            r['family']='Pouch' if cap==10 else 'Backpack'
    return lw.inventory(transformed,bearer)

def wearing_eligible(r):
    return (r.get('state')=='worn' and r.get('complete',False)
        and r.get('hp',0)>=1 and not r.get('suppressed',False)
        and r.get('fit',False) and r.get('slotValid',False))

def validate_designated_slots(items):
    occupied={}
    for r in items:
        # Broken and suppressed bodies do not automatically vacate a designation.
        if r.get('state')!='worn' or not r.get('slotValid'):continue
        slot=r.get('slot')
        if r.get('trade')=='Tailoring' and r.get('family')!='Bag':
            if slot!=SLOTS[r['family']]:raise ValueError('incorrect Tailoring position')
        key=(r.get('wearer'),slot)
        if key in occupied:raise ValueError('competing designated benefits')
        occupied[key]=r['id']
    return occupied

def tailoring_task_bonus(items,task):
    """itemPremises is keyed by physical item ID; omitted premises fail closed.

    The governing task procedure establishes each item's contribution, coverage,
    visibility, access relationship and domain criteria independently. Shared
    task fields describe only the check, actor, ability, proficiency and domain.
    This helper does not establish physical circumstances or equipping legality.
    """
    if not task.get('actualCheck') or task.get('kind')!='ability-check':return 0
    domain=task['domain']
    ability=task['ability'];proficiency=task.get('proficiency')
    scores=[]
    for r in items:
        if r.get('trade')!='Tailoring' or domain not in FAMILY_TASKS[r['family']]:continue
        own=task.get('itemPremises',{}).get(r['id'],{})
        if not own.get('domainCriteriaMet') or not own.get('actualContribution'):continue
        valid={
          'presentation':(ability=='CHA' and proficiency in ('Persuasion','Deception','Performance') and own.get('visibleAttire')) or (ability=='DEX' and proficiency=='disguise-kit' and own.get('ownAlreadyWornArrangement')),
          'visual-concealment':ability=='DEX' and proficiency=='Stealth' and own.get('legalHiding'),
          'quiet-clothing-motion':ability=='DEX' and proficiency=='Stealth',
          'discreet-handwork':ability=='DEX' and proficiency=='Sleight of Hand',
          'weather-arrangement':(ability=='WIS' and proficiency=='Survival') or (ability=='DEX' and own.get('ownOrdinaryFastenings')),
          'exposure':ability=='CON' and own.get('independentExposureProcedureAllowsClothing'),
          'conceal-contents':ability=='DEX' and own.get('noncreatureCargo'),
          'organize-contents':ability in ('INT','DEX') and own.get('noncreatureCargo')}
        if not valid.get(domain,False):continue
        if r['family']=='Bag':
            eligible=(r.get('complete') and r.get('hp',0)>=1 and not r.get('suppressed')
              and r.get('state')!='melded' and r.get('accessible')
              and r.get('operator')==task.get('actor'))
        else:
            eligible=wearing_eligible(r) and r.get('wearer')==task.get('actor') and own.get('coverageSufficient')
        if eligible:scores.append(r['bonus'])
    return max(scores,default=0)

def cross_trade_task_bonus(items,task,leather_task_ids=()):
    """Validate all designations before item-specific filtering for one check.

    leather_task_ids describes the actual check; each physical item's own
    leatherTaskIds must additionally qualify. Owner/wearer is not an operator
    fallback for a container. Reviewed Leatherworking rules remain unchanged.
    """
    validate_designated_slots(items)
    tailoring=tailoring_task_bonus(items,task)
    leather=[r for r in items if r.get('trade')=='Leatherworking']
    leather_score=0
    if task.get('kind')=='ability-check' and task.get('actualCheck'):
        for r in leather:
            own=task.get('itemPremises',{}).get(r['id'],{})
            container=r['family'] in ('Backpack','Pouch','Quiver','Belt','Harness')
            actor=r.get('operator') if container else r.get('wearer')
            if actor!=task.get('actor') or not own.get('leatherCriteriaMet') or not own.get('actualContribution'):continue
            ids=set(leather_task_ids).intersection(own.get('leatherTaskIds',()))
            leather_score=max(leather_score,lw.task_bonus([r],True,ids,own.get('noncreatureCargo',False)))
    return max(tailoring,leather_score)

def defense(suit_grade,items):
    validate_designated_slots(items)
    # Tailoring is absent from the AC candidate set, including when all items fail.
    rest=[r for r in items if r.get('trade')!='Tailoring']
    result=lw.defense(suit_grade,rest)
    resistances=set(result['resistances'])
    for r in items:
        if r.get('trade')=='Tailoring' and wearing_eligible(r) and r.get('resistance'):
            resistances.add(r['resistance'])
    result['resistances']=sorted(resistances)
    return result

def repair_terms(current,maximum,base_value,finished_value,dc,original_body=True,
                 required_constituents_present=True):
    """Quotation only, never permission to begin repair.

    Both repair routes require training AND actual Weaver's tools (Tailor's-tools
    binding), actual Loom and Cutting Table, Needles and Shears. An implement may
    satisfy compatible overlapping requirements; no duplicate purchase is needed.
    A quotation does not satisfy a missing toolkit or other physical prerequisite.
    Rare source creation-tool rows are not additional generic repair requirements.
    """
    if not required_constituents_present:raise ValueError('missing original member/constituent')
    return lw.repair_terms(current,maximum,base_value,finished_value,dc,recoverable=original_body)

def tool_reservation(tool_id,active_order_ids,new_order_id,qualified_profile):
    if not qualified_profile:raise ValueError('source profile or legal substitute absent')
    if set(active_order_ids)-{new_order_id}:raise ValueError('tool reserved for overlapping work')
    return {'physicalToolId':tool_id,'orderId':new_order_id}

def creation_minutes(source_hours,forge_dwarf=False):
    actual=F(1920) if source_hours is None else F(str(source_hours))
    return ceil(actual*(45 if forge_dwarf else 60))

def purchase_cp(reference_gp):
    return ceil(F(str(reference_gp))*100)


UNCHANGED REVIEWED LEATHERWORKING DEPENDENCY

"""Bounded reference calculations for the authored Leatherworking manuscript.

Not the companion runtime. Physical fit/anatomy/permissions are explicit premises.
Box helpers prove specified orthogonal packing only, not arbitrary 3-D fitting.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import permutations
from copy import deepcopy

SLOT_FAMILY={'back-cargo':{'Backpack','Harness'},'quiver':{'Quiver'},
             'waist-cargo':{'Belt'},'pouch-1':{'Pouch'},'pouch-2':{'Pouch'}}

def number(n): return F(str(n))

def custody(records):
    """Require an acyclic forest with one node and one parent per physical ID."""
    by={r['id']:r for r in records}
    if len(by)!=len(records): raise ValueError('duplicate physical identity')
    parent={}
    for r in records:
        if r.get('inclusiveGross') and r.get('contents'): raise ValueError('inclusive record also expanded')
        for child in r.get('contents',[]):
            if child not in by: raise ValueError('missing physical record')
            if child in parent: raise ValueError('multiple custody parents')
            parent[child]=r['id']
    done=set();visiting=set()
    def visit(id):
        if id in visiting: raise ValueError('cyclic custody')
        if id in done: return
        visiting.add(id)
        for child in by[id].get('contents',[]):visit(child)
        visiting.remove(id);done.add(id)
    for id in by:visit(id)
    return by,parent

def subtree(by,id):
    yield id
    for child in by[id].get('contents',[]):yield from subtree(by,child)

def inventory(records,bearer):
    by,parent=custody(records)
    roots=[r['id'] for r in records if r['id'] not in parent and r.get('bearer')==bearer]
    allids=[id for root in roots for id in subtree(by,root)]
    active=[];occupied={}
    for id in roots:
        r=by[id];slot=r.get('slot')
        # Invalid/incomplete held/worn assemblies never create magical permission.
        equipped=(r.get('state') in ('worn','held') and r.get('fit',False)
                  and (r.get('state')!='held' or r.get('handsSatisfied',False)))
        if equipped and slot in SLOT_FAMILY and r.get('family') in SLOT_FAMILY[slot]:
            if slot in occupied: raise ValueError('two occupants designated in one relief slot')
            occupied[slot]=id
            if r.get('hp',0)>=1 and not r.get('suppressed') and r.get('complete',True) and 'relief' in r: active.append(id)
    pounds=F(0);load=F(0);unknown=[]
    for id in allids:
        r=by[id];load+=number(r['load'])
        if r.get('weight') is None:unknown.append(id)
        else:pounds+=number(r['weight'])
    coverage={};credits={}
    for id in active:
        allowance=number(by[id]['relief'])
        for child in by[id].get('contents',[]):
            for cid in subtree(by,child):
                r=by[cid]
                if r.get('creatureBody') or r.get('weight') is None: continue
                # Every descendant belongs to only this root; body precedes children.
                covered=min(allowance,number(r['weight']));allowance-=covered
                coverage[cid]=covered;credits[cid]=min(number(r['load']),covered/10)
    return {'physicalPounds':None if unknown else pounds,'knownPoundsSubtotal':pounds,
      'canonicalLoad':load,'unknownWeightIds':unknown,'activeContainers':active,
      'coveredPoundsById':coverage,'loadCreditById':credits,
      'countedPounds':None if unknown else pounds-sum(coverage.values(),F(0)),
      'effectiveLoad':load-sum(credits.values(),F(0)),'physicalIds':allids}

def transfer(records,root,new_bearer,state='carried',slot=None,fit=False,hands=False):
    result=deepcopy(records);by,parent=custody(result)
    if root in parent: raise ValueError('remove from containing custody before direct transfer')
    by[root].update(bearer=new_bearer,state=state,slot=slot,fit=fit,handsSatisfied=hands)
    return result

def break_container(records,id,retained=(),same_location_legal=True,adjacent_legal=True):
    """Placement premises are supplied by the reviewed board/placement system."""
    result=deepcopy(records);by,parent=custody(result);r=by[id];r['hp']=0
    old=list(r.get('contents',[]));r['contents']=[]
    # retained maps children to independently recorded supporting parent IDs.
    retained=dict(retained);released=[]
    for child in old:
        if child in retained:
            support=by[retained[child]]
            if support['id']==id or support.get('hp',0)<1 or not support.get('independentSupport'):
                raise ValueError('no genuine independent intact restraint')
            support.setdefault('contents',[]).append(child);continue
        by[child].update(bearer=None,slot=None,state='placed' if same_location_legal or adjacent_legal else 'pending-placement',
                         location=r.get('location') if same_location_legal else 'nearest-legal-adjacent' if adjacent_legal else None)
        released.append(child)
    custody(result)
    return result,released

def maximum_hp(ordinary,percent=0,flawed=False):
    if ordinary<1 or percent not in (0,25,50):raise ValueError('invalid structural layer')
    improved=ordinary+(max(1,ordinary*percent//100) if percent else 0)
    return max(1,improved//2) if flawed else improved

def apply_quality(ordinary,old_percent,old_flaw,old_current,new_percent=0,new_flaw=False):
    oldmax=maximum_hp(ordinary,old_percent,old_flaw)
    if not 0<=old_current<=oldmax:raise ValueError('invalid current structural HP')
    p=max(old_percent,new_percent);flaw=old_flaw or new_flaw;newmax=maximum_hp(ordinary,p,flaw)
    return {'ordinaryHP':ordinary,'percent':p,'flaw':flaw,'maximum':newmax,'current':max(0,newmax-(oldmax-old_current))}

def complete_order(reserves,q,outcome,option='yield'):
    """Reserves are the latest lawful completion records in predeclared order."""
    if len(reserves)!=3*q//2:raise ValueError('wrong maximum reserve count')
    if len({r['id'] for r in reserves})!=len(reserves):raise ValueError('shared body')
    for r in reserves:
        if r.get('missing') or r.get('committedElsewhere') or r.get('imprint') or not r.get('complete',True) or r['current']<1 or r.get('contents'):
            raise ValueError('pause before completion check')
    if len({(r['model'],r['size'],r['grade']) for r in reserves})!=1:raise ValueError('batch model size grade differs')
    n={'Standard':q,'Exceptional':5*q//4,'Masterwork':3*q//2,'Flawed':q//2 if q>=2 else 1,'Failed':0,'Mishap':0}[outcome]
    pct=0
    if outcome in ('Exceptional','Masterwork') and option=='durability':n=q;pct=25 if outcome=='Exceptional' else 50
    output=[]
    for r in reserves[:n]:
        z=deepcopy(r);z.update(apply_quality(r['ordinaryHP'],r['percent'],r['flaw'],r['current'],pct,outcome=='Flawed' and q==1));z['imprint']=True;output.append(z)
    return output,deepcopy(reserves[n:])

def repair_terms(current,maximum,base_value,finished_value,dc,pair=False,recoverable=True,both_members=True):
    if not recoverable or pair and not both_members:raise ValueError('generic reconstruction unavailable')
    if not 0<=current<=maximum:raise ValueError('invalid HP')
    return {'DC':10 if current>=1 else dc,'hours':max(F(1 if current>=1 else 8),F(maximum,10)),
            'materialsGp':number(base_value if current>=1 else finished_value)/4,'restoredMaximum':maximum}

def defense(suit_grade,items):
    """Compare eligible enhancement candidates; this is not an equipment resolver.

    suit_grade is the already-validated grade modifier of the complete,
    functional, actually worn suit whose armor formula is selected, or None
    when no such modifier is eligible. The caller establishes that eligibility
    and any permission to use an alternative AC formula while wearing armor.
    """
    candidates=[] if suit_grade is None else [suit_grade];res=set()
    for r in items:
        worn=r.get('state')=='worn' and r.get('fit') and r.get('complete') and r.get('hp',0)>=1 and not r.get('suppressed') and (r.get('family')=='Armor' or r.get('slotValid'))
        if not worn:continue
        if r.get('resistance'):res.add(r['resistance'])
        if r.get('armorBonus',0)>0 and (r.get('family')!='Armor' or r.get('selectedArmorFormula')):
            candidates.append(r['armorBonus'])
    return {'enhancement':max(candidates) if candidates else None,'resistances':sorted(res)}

TASK_IDS={
 'Boots':{'foothold-climb','checked-jump','foot-balance'},
 'Gloves':{'handhold-climb','rope-grip','manual-cargo-retention','manual-cargo-position','nonlocking-fitting','jammed-small-object'},
 'Leather Gear':{'climb','checked-cramped-passage'},
 'Armor':{'climb','checked-cramped-passage'},'Coat':{'climb','checked-cramped-passage'},
 'container':{'secure-closure','cargo-handling','unlocked-retrieval','jammed-content','tangled-content'}}

def task_bonus(items,actual_check,task_ids,physical_noncreature=True):
    if not actual_check or not physical_noncreature:return 0
    candidates=[]
    for r in items:
        family=r['family'];container=family in ('Backpack','Pouch','Quiver','Belt','Harness')
        state=(r.get('accessible') and r.get('state')!='melded') if container else (r.get('state')=='worn' and r.get('slotValid') and r.get('fit'))
        if state and r.get('complete') and r.get('hp',0)>=1 and not r.get('suppressed') and TASK_IDS['container' if container else family].intersection(task_ids):candidates.append(r['bonus'])
    return max(candidates,default=0)

def orthogonal_box_fit(dimensions,interior,opening,opening_axes=(1,2)):
    """Existence proof for a rigid box in one orthogonal loading orientation."""
    return any(all(a<=b for a,b in zip(p,interior)) and p[opening_axes[0]]<=opening[0] and p[opening_axes[1]]<=opening[1]
               for p in set(permutations(dimensions)))

def compartment_limits(model,placements):
    """Check explicit non-overlapping orthogonal placements; no packing search."""
    count=model['compartmentCount'];totw=F(0);totv=F(0)
    volume=lambda d:F(d['numerator'],d['denominator'])
    for i in range(count):
        ps=[p for p in placements if p['compartment']==i]
        if any(not orthogonal_box_fit(p['dimensions'],model['interiorInches'],model['openingInches'],model['openingAxes']) for p in ps):return False
        if any(any(x<0 or x+d>limit for x,d,limit in zip(p['position'],p['dimensions'],model['interiorInches'])) for p in ps):return False
        for a_index,a in enumerate(ps):
            for b in ps[a_index+1:]:
                if all(a['position'][k]<b['position'][k]+b['dimensions'][k] and b['position'][k]<a['position'][k]+a['dimensions'][k] for k in range(3)):return False
        w=sum((number(p['weight']) for p in ps),F(0));v=sum((number(p['volumeFt3']) for p in ps),F(0))
        if w>model['compartmentWeightLimitLb'] or v>volume(model['compartmentVolumeFt3']):return False
        totw+=w;totv+=v
    return (all(0<=p['compartment']<count for p in placements) and totw<=model['physicalContentWeightLimitLb'] and totv<=volume(model['physicalContentVolumeFt3']))

def meld_preflight(records,root_ids,pound_ceiling,load_ceiling):
    by,_=custody(records);seen=set()
    for id in root_ids:
        ids=set(subtree(by,id))
        if seen.intersection(ids):raise ValueError('subtree selected twice')
        seen.update(ids)
    if any(by[id].get('weight') is None for id in seen):raise ValueError('known mass required for meld preflight')
    pounds=sum((number(by[id]['weight']) for id in seen),F(0));load=sum((number(by[id]['load']) for id in seen),F(0))
    return {'pounds':pounds,'load':load,'fits':pounds<=number(pound_ceiling) and load<=number(load_ceiling),'physicalIds':sorted(seen)}

    def check(name,result,detail=None):
        checks.append({'name':name,'pass':bool(result),**({'detail':detail} if detail is not None else {})})
        assert result,name
    def rejected(name,fn):
        try:fn()
        except ValueError:check(name,True);return
        check(name,False)
    def jewel(n,physical=None,**physical_state):

        z.update(physical_state)

    # Structural matrix is independent of all charge ledgers.

    recipe=cards['recipe-245']['sourceRecipe'];reserves=[{'id':f'bracelet-{i}','model':'jewelcrafting.bracelet','size':'Medium','grade':'Standard','ordinaryHP':5,'maximum':5,'current':5,'percent':0,'flaw':False,'complete':True,'available':True} for i in range(7)];ids=[r['id'] for r in reserves]

    kit={k:True for k in ('trained','actualToolkit','actualBench','actualFineFiles','actualLens','recoverableOriginalPrimary','requiredOriginalStonesSettingsPresent','originalCrystal','originalPrimaryMount')};kit['ability']='INT'
    earring=jewel(242);quote=ref.repair_quote(earring,F(1,2),F(265,2),12,kit);check('Earring functional repair0.125gp purchase0.13',quote['materialsGp']==F(1,8) and math.ceil(quote['materialsGp']*100)==13)
    earring['hp']=0;quote=ref.repair_quote(earring,F(1,2),F(265,2),12,kit);check('Earring reconstruction33.125gp purchase33.13',quote['materialsGp']==F(265,8) and math.ceil(quote['materialsGp']*100)==3313 and quote['DC']==12)

    # Cross-trade fixtures come from the exact reviewed cards, not invented bonuses.
